In radical SAM enzymes, the [4Fe-4S] cluster at the radical SAM site is coordinated by 3 Cys residues from the CX3CX2C motif, with the 4th coordination site being the SAM substrate.

In other [4Fe-4S] sites (like the auxiliary clusters in radical SAM enzymes), the cluster is coordinated by 4 Cys residues.

So if the question is about the radical SAM [4Fe-4S] cluster, the answer would be 3 cysteines from a CxxxCxxC-like motif. If it&#x27;s about an auxiliary cluster, it would be 4 cysteines.
